= [b for b in bullets\n+                     if not is_gate_rule(b) and any(k in b for k in (\"前面\", \"因为\", \"记得\", \"经历\"))]\n+            if rule_hits:\n+                for b in rule_hits:\n+                    items.append({\"station\": title, \"text\": b[2:].strip() if b.startswith(\"- \") else b.strip()})\n+            elif fuzzy:\n+                needs_model.append((w, title))\n+    return control, needs_model\n+\n+\n+# ---------- 珠子映射 ----------\n+\n+def mm_range(s):\n+    m = re.search(r\"mm\\.(\\d+)-(\\d+)\", s)\n+    if m:\n+        return int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))\n+    m = re.search(r\"m\\.(\\d+)\", s)\n+    if m:\n+        return int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(1))\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def map_station_to_bead(work, station_title, beads):\n+    \"\"\"按小节范围把每一站对应到重叠最多的那一颗珠子；对不上的返回 None。\"\"\"\n+    r = mm_range(station_title)\n+    if r is None:\n+        return None, \"标题无小节范围\"\n+    a, b = r\n+    best, best_ov = None, -1\n+    for bead in beads:\n+        br = mm_range(bead[\"title\"] + \" \" + bead[\"text\"][:200])\n+        if br is None:\n+            br = mm_range(bead[\"title\"])\n+        if br is None:\n+            continue\n+        c, d = br\n+        ov = max(0, min(b, d) - max(a, c) + 1)\n+        if ov > best_ov:\n+            best, best_ov = bead, ov\n+    if best is None or best_ov <= 0:\n+        return None, \"无重叠珠子\"\n+    return best, None\n+\n+\n+# ---------- 主流程 ----------\n+\n+def gen_chain(args):\n+    work, writer, beads_w, chain_w, cached, calls = args\n+    recs = []\n+    for b in recs.append(rec)\n+            print(\"[gen %s/%s k%d] cached ok\" % (work, writer, k), flush=True)\n+            continue\n+        src = chain_w.get(k, {})\n+        inv = \"work_key\": work, \"work_name\": WORKS[work], \"sample\": 1,\n+               \"extra\": {\"writer\": writer, \"k\": k},\n+               \"forbid_tools\": list(FORBID), \"_norm\": norm_gen}\n+        rec_call, _ = do_call(dict(job), RUN_DIR, calls, \"gen\")\n+        rec = {\"work\": work, \"writer\": writer, \"k\": k,\n+               \"title\": b[\"title\"], \"bead\": b[\"text\"],\n+               \"inventory_before\": inv, \"prompt\": prompt,\n+               \"job_id\": job[\"job_id\"], \"attempts\": rec_call[\"attempts\"],\n+               \"output\": rec_call[\"output\"], \"parsed\":  print(\"[gen %s/%s k%d] ok=%s\" % (work, writer, k, rec[\"parse_ok\"]), flush=True)\n+    return recs\n+\n+\n+def judge_chain(args):\n+    group, work, sent_idx, bead_text, sentence, judge, cached,         \"- 评判调用（含解析重试）：%d\" % c[\"judge_calls\"],\n+          \"- 总调用数：%d\" % c[\"total\"],\n+          \"- 最终失败的生成：%s\" % (\"无\" if not c[\"failed_gen\"] else \"、\".join(c[\"failed_gen\"])),\n+          \"- 最终失败的评判：%s\" % (\"无\" if not c[\"failed_judge\"] else \"、\".join(c[\"failed_judge\"])),\n+          \"\"]\n+    # 1. 生成统计\n+    L += [\"## 1. 生成统计（先判断再写）\", \"\",\n+          \"| 作品 × 写的人 | 段数 | differs=true 段数 | 无的比例 |\",\n+          \"|---|---|---|---|\"]\n+    for w in WORKS_LIST:\n+        for writer in MODELS_GEN:\n+            recs = sorted([r for r in gen_recs if r[\"work\"] == w and |\n120: | 第 7 段 | mm.93回来的长连歌声听起来已不是最初有所保留不敢放开的轻唱，也不是随后加花变密越甜越喘的紧抓，而是位置更高、声音更实的松开唱。 | 从mm.5开口时克制保留、到mm.27加花变密越抓越喘的那道旋律，在经历翻浪后的大喊扑腾挣扎后，于mm.93以更高、更实的声音回来时，听起来不再是焦灼用力地去抓，而是褪去了慌乱、带着所有疼过喘过的痕迹沉着立住的放开与尊严。 | | 只听这一段的人只听到水花碎光的轻巧跳跃，而记得前面变化的人听 mm.103-111 那段像碎光跳跃的极轻音色，听出的是从 m.62 满到溢出翻船的亮、到浪里乱抓时划不回来的亮，在此刻变成了彻底放下抓握与防备、受过伤却不再沉重的释然之光。 | 不同：mm.103–111 轻跳的高音，会让人听见 mm.5–26 克制的长唱、mm.27–43 加花变密的急促、mm.93–102
